## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 11 page(s) - 11 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: ALFA
**Rule**: [SIA-R47: No description](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r47)
**Issue**: The `<meta>` element restricts the ability to zoom

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0,
                                 maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no" />
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
/meta
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('/meta')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://www.wfp.org/eu2021
- https://www.wfp.org/emergencies/drc-emergency
- https://www.wfp.org/countries/china
- https://www.wfp.org/news/funding-crunch-deprives-120000-children-school-meals-drought-ridden-southern-madagascar
- https://www.wfp.org/countries/myanmar
- *...and 6 more page(s)*

## Detailed Failure Information (ALFA)

### https://www.wfp.org/

#### Rule: [SIA-R111: Interactive elements have a sufficient target size](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r111)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.5.5](https://www.w3.org/TR/WCAG22/) (Level AAA)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-08-05
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a href="https://www.wfp.org/stories/colliding-crises-leave-afghanistans-children-brink-malnutrition-emergency" class="button-new button-new--primary">READ MORE</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="button-new button-new--primary"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-08-05
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a href="https://donate.wfp.org/1243/donation/regular/?campaign=4580&survey=true" class="button-new button-new--danger--primary" data-once="button-donate">DONATE</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="button-new button-new--danger--primary"]`

**Failure 3:**
- First identified: 2026-08-05
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<div class="hero-carousel__swiper-button-next" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Showing next slide">...</div>`
- XPath: `/div[@class="hero-carousel__swiper-button-next"]`

**Failure 4:**
- First identified: 2026-08-05
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<div class="latest-good__swiper-button-next" tabindex="0" role="button" aria-label="Showing next slide">...</div>`
- XPath: `/div[@class="latest-good__swiper-button-next"]`

**Failure 5:**
- First identified: 2026-08-05
- Message: Target has insufficient size
- HTML: `<a data-component-id="wfp_org:button" href="https://www.wfp.org/governments" class="button-new">Explore cooperation</a>`
- XPath: `/a[@class="button-new"]`

*... and 5 more failures for this rule*

#### Rule: [SIA-R14: The visible label text of an element is included in its accessible name](https://alfa.siteimprove.com/rules/sia-r14)
**WCAG**: [SC 2.5.3](https://www.w3.org/WAI/WCAG22/Understanding/label-in-name) (Level A)

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-08-05
- Message: The visible text content of the element is not included within its accessible name
- HTML: `<a href="https://x.com/WFP" title="X" aria-label="Visit WFP X channel" target="_blank">Visit X</a>`
- XPath: `/a`

**WCAG**: Best Practice

## Detailed Failure Information (axe)

### https://www.wfp.org/

#### Rule: [aria-allowed-role](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.12/aria-allowed-role?application=playwright)
**Impact**: minor

**Failure 1:**
- First identified: 2026-08-05
- Message: ARIA role should be appropriate for the element
- HTML: `<article class="swiper-slide swiper-slide-active" data-hero-slide="0" style="--hero-carousel__slide-opacity: 0.7; width: 1280px;" role="group" aria-label="1 / 3">`
- XPath: `article[data-hero-slide="0"]`

**Failure 2:**
- First identified: 2026-08-05
- Message: ARIA role should be appropriate for the element
- HTML: `<video data-js-hook="hero-carousel__video--desktop" role="none" preload="none" class="hero-carousel__video hero-carousel__video--desktop" playsinline="" muted="" loop="" poster="https://www.wfp.org/sites/default/files/2026-07/Sediqa%27s%20three%20year%20old.jpeg" autoplay="">`

- HTML: `<h3 id="related--2" class="publication-teaser-related__title">           After-Action Review: Leveraging 4Ps Programme for Emergency Response         </h3>`
- XPath: `#related--2`

**Failure 4:**

- Message: Elements must meet minimum color contrast ratio thresholds
- HTML: `<h3 id="related--3" class="publication-teaser-related__title">           Fill the Nutrient Gap - Asia and the Pacific         </h3>`
- XPath: `#related--3`

**Failure 5:**

- HTML: `<h3 id="related--4" class="publication-teaser-related__title">           2024 Typhoon Season: WFP Philippines Response Report         </h3>`
